$ artgen dungeon new --root <root>
threw: dungeon new: <id> is required

$ artgen dungeon new bad --room poly --root <root>
threw: --room: rect, circle, octagon or cave

$ artgen dungeon new bad --room rect --room-size 6 --root <root>
threw: --room-size: WIDTHxHEIGHT in cells, e.g. 8x6

$ artgen dungeon new bad --room rect --exits x --root <root>
threw: --exits: sides n, e, s, w, each optionally :door kind and :a note (n,e:secret,s:none:To the caves)

$ artgen dungeon new bad --layout maze --root <root>
threw: --layout: scatter, dense, blocks

$ artgen dungeon link crypt r1 --root <root>
threw: dungeon link <id> <room|corridor:id> <room|x,y|C4|corridor:id>

$ artgen dungeon link crypt r1 r9 --route sideways --root <root>
threw: --route: auto, hv, vh, straight, around

$ artgen dungeon link crypt r1 r9 --curve wavy --root <root>
threw: --curve: none, smooth

$ artgen dungeon link crypt r1 r9 --style rough --root <root>
threw: --style: built, natural

$ artgen dungeon link crypt r1 r9 --doors gate --root <root>
threw: --doors: none or one of door, secret, locked, portcullis, arch

$ artgen dungeon link crypt r1 r9 --exit x --root <root>
threw: --exit: the second end must be a point (x,y or a cell name), where the corridor leaves the map

$ artgen dungeon link crypt r1 r9 --from-side x --root <root>
threw: --from-side / --to-side: n, e, s, w

$ artgen dungeon link crypt r1 r9 --via 1,2,3 --root <root>
threw: --via: comma-separated numbers in x,y pairs

$ artgen settlement new mill --root <root>
threw: settlement mill already exists (edit it, or pick another id)

$ artgen settlement new --root <root>
threw: settlement new: <id> is required

$ artgen settlement new bad --kind metropolis --root <root>
threw: --kind: hamlet, village, town, city, castle

$ artgen settlement new bad --map nope --root <root>
threw: --map: no map nope (art/maps/nope/map.json)

$ artgen settlement new bad --size 5 --root <root>
threw: --size: WIDTHxHEIGHT in cells, e.g. 8x6

# torchdeep: pipeline analytics

10 assets. Price basis: $4/M input (review images, measured input), $20/M output (code; estimated at 3.5 chars/token unless measured).

Totals: 14818 output tok, 45725 review-image tok, est. $0.479. Average gain from revisions +1.3, from the finishing pass +0.1.

The cost leaves out what the agents read (skills, direction, rigs): pass `score --agent-tokens-in <n>` to count it.
